The civilian dimension of security, as a security concept, will make it more important with the pandemic we live in, as conventional wars and threats are replaced by different kinds of wars and threats. In conventional wars, people of certain professions, age groups and genders are affected, while today societies suffer deep damage regardless of the new type of economic, cyber -- if true -- biological threats, gender, and occupation. The nature of the epidemic is that agriculture is seriously affected. In particular, many food exporters are struggling to find agricultural workers who will remove the harvest, distribute the harvest, and produce new crops. Agriculture's organisation, agriculture technologies, and agricultural product diversity have become part of food security, and their absence may lead to famine rather than a national security issue.
